Gradient wrt h_i for positive lags: For pair (i,j=i+m): contribution to f_p: h_i - h_i h_j. d/dh_i = 1 - h_j. For negative lag p, pair (i,j=i+p): f_{-p}=h_j - h_i h_j; d/dh_i = -h_j, d/dh_j = 1 - h_i. Also h_i appears as left element in many positive lags (to right neighbors) and as right element in negative lags (to left neighbors). We can compute gradient by convolution-like: For each lag p positive with weight w_p, contribution to grad[0:n-p] += w_p(1-h[p:n]); grad[p:n]-=w_p*h[0:n-p] for f_p; for f_{-p}: grad[p:n]+=w_p*h[0:n-p]; grad[0:n-p]-=w_p*h[p:n]? Wait f_{-p} terms sum_{i=0}^{L-1} h_{i+p} - h_i h_{i+p}; derivative wrt h_i (i left) = -h_{i+p}; derivative wrt h_j (j right) = 1 - h_{i}. So grad[i] += -w h_{i+p}; grad[i+p] += w(1-h_i). Sum with f_p: grad[i] += w(1-h_j); grad[j] += -w h_i; plus f_{-p}: grad[i] -= w h_j; grad[j]+=w - w h_i. Total for pair i<j distance p: grad[i] += w(1-h_j) - w h_j = w(1 - 2h_j). grad[j] += -w h_i + w(1-h_i) = w(1 - 2h_i). Nice if we include both symmetric lags equal weight. Algorithm `direction_search`:
- Compute weights from smooth max (α maybe 1e4) and gradient g (true subgradient of F? We can compute exact subgradient for current active max set: average gradient over lags with c within tol; or use smooth weights). Since F is max of C_m, subgradient is convex combo over active. Smooth weights approximate.
- Direction: For feasible moves preserving sum, allowable vector v with ∑v=0, and bounds. The most negative directional derivative g·v. A simple choice: pair i (min g) and j (max g), move mass t from j to i if g_i < g_j? Actually objective change ≈ g·v. To decrease, move mass from high gradient to low gradient: h_i += t, h_j -= t; v_i=1, v_j=-1, derivative g_i - g_j <0.
- Perform exact line search over t in interval ensuring bounds: t ∈ [min_t, max_t], h_i+t, h_j-t. Since one feasible segment. Evaluate true C and find minimum via ternary/golden (F not convex in t because max of quadratics but maybe unimodal? Could be not; use sampling + golden or scipy minimize_scalar bounded with 40 evals). For n=300, each eval O(n) correlate? 40* many pairs.
- We can process all i? Too many. Use top K imbalances? For each i, best j maybe argmax g with enough mass. Use limited pairs per iteration.
- This is like coordinate Newton.

Let's detail functions:
- `compute_c_full(h)`: np.correlate(h, 1-h, mode='full') * dx, return c, cmax.
- `feasible_project(h, n)`: ensure sum n/2, bounds. Use iterative waterfill to exactly target. We can implement robust:
   h = np.clip(h,0,1)
   for iterations: diff=target-h.sum(); if abs diff < 1e-12 break; if diff>0: add to elements with room, proportional to room? previous adds diff/tot*room. This maintains bounds if diff/tot ≤1? diff ≤ tot? target ≤ n, sum≤n, diff can be >0 only if sum<target; room=sum(1-h)=n-sum; diff=target-sum ≤ n-sum=room. diff/tot≤1, adding proportional doesn't exceed bounds. If diff<0: tot=sum; ratio = 1+diff/tot = target/sum, multiply h by ratio. Iterations couple? - `project_feasible(h, target=None)`:
```
def project_feasible(h, target=None):
    n=len(h); h=np.ascontiguousarray(h,dtype=float); h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    if target is None: target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(1000):
       s=h.sum()
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-13*max(1.0,n): break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<=1e-14: break
          h += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          if s<=1e-14: break
          h *= (target/s)
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    # exact final adjust
    for _ in range(20):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
       if diff>0: idx=np.argmax(1.0-h); h[idx]=min(1.0,h[idx]+diff)
       else: idx=np.argmax(h); h[idx]=max(0.0,h[idx]+diff)
    return h
```
But if diff large, adjusting one may clip; loop. Use while.

Maybe `project_feasible` using PCHIP? no.

```
def smooth_grad(h,n,alpha,mu=0.0):
   c=...; m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); amax=z.max(); z-=amax; w=np.exp(z); w /= w.sum();
   g=np.zeros(n); center=n-1; dx=2/n;
   wc=w[center]
   if wc>0: g += wc*(1-2*h)
   for p in range(1,n):
       wp=w[center-p]; wn=w[center+p]
       if wp>1e-15 or wn>1e-15:
          L=n-p
          hp=h[p:]; hl=h[:L]
          if wp>1e-15:
             g[:L] += wp*(1-hp)
             g[p:] -= wp*hl
          if wn>1e-15:
             g[:L] -= wn*hp
             g[p:] += wn*(1-hl)
   g *= dx
   if mu: g += 2*mu*(h.sum()-n/2)*np.ones(n)
   return g
```
For p loop, if wp/wn extremely small skip. Use `wp > 1e-20`.

Need ensure `c-m` may have zeros; z can be -inf? no.

L-BFGS phases:
```
def lbfgs_optimize(h,n,deadline,alphas=None,mus=None,maxiter=200):
    h=project_feasible(h)
    best_c=eval_cmax(h); best_h=h.copy()
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    for alpha, mu in zip(...):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       res = minimize(obj_pen, h, jac=grad_pen, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol':1e-18,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':maxiter*5,'maxls':30})
       h2=project_feasible(res.x); c2=eval_cmax(h2); if c2<best_c: update
    return best_h,best_c
```

`grad_pen(x,n,alpha,mu)` similar.

```
def pair_line_search(h,n,i,j,best_c):
   if i==j: return h,best_c,False
   lo=max(-h[i], h[j]-1.0)
   hi=min(h[j], 1.0-h[i])
   if hi-lo < 1e-12: return ...
   # If current t=0 is in bounds by definition. Evaluate endpoints and some sample
   # Use 20 log? Actually t range maybe [-0.5,0.5]. Sample linspace.
   vals = np.linspace(lo,hi,20)
   best_t=0; best_val=best_c
   for t in vals: if valid: modify, compute, restore.
   # golden around bracket containing best? We know interval, but F not convex. Use local interval [best_t - step, best_t + step] within bounds, with width maybe (hi-lo)/10.
   # Use scipy optimize minimize_scalar? Top-level? Simpler implement own parabolic/golden with 25 iterations bounded [lo_r,hi_r].
```

```
       neg_idx = np.argsort(grad)[:K]
       pos_idx = np.argsort(grad)[-K:][::-1]
       improved=False
       for pi in pos_idx:

    # if best_c improved to target? break
```

`direction_polish(h,n,deadline,best_c,seed=0)`:
Pseudo:
```
def direction_polish(h,n,deadline,best_c,seed=0):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    best = best_c
    alphas = [50,200,1000,5000,20000]
    K=6
    for alpha in alphas:
       improved_round=True
       while time.time() < deadline and improved_round:
          improved_round=False
          c=compute_c_full(h)
          m=c.max()
          # active weights; if alpha finite use softmax else uniform over c>m-1e-7
          z=alpha*(c-m); amax=z.max(); z-=amax; w=exp(z); w/=w.sum()
          g=grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
          # If all diff small break
          neg_idx=np.argsort(g)[:K]; pos_idx=np.argsort(g)[-K:][::-1]
          # line search for pairs. Add maybe swapped (positive to negative) only.
          for pi in pos_idx:
             for ni in neg_idx:
                if pi==ni: continue
                # optional: if g[pi]-g[ni] <= 1e-8: continue
                h_new, c_new = pair_line_search(h,n,pi,ni,best)
                if c_new < best - 1e-13:
                   h=h_new; best=c_new; improved_round=True
          # If no improvement, try random pairs 30
          if not improved_round:
             rng=np.random.RandomState(seed + int(alpha))
             for _ in range(30):
                i=rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
                h_new,c_new=pair_line_search(...); update
             if improved_round: pass
             else: break
    # final small local pair step with tiny line search? 
    return h,best
```
Line search function takes current h and returns modified copy? To avoid modifying and failing restore, we can pass h and inside use temp modifications and restore on each eval; at end if improve, set values. It returns best_c but not best_h? We can return h modified if success.

Potential line search quality: Uniform 24 + golden 12. For t range, the best may be at boundary; include endpoints. Evaluate coarsely 16 maybe enough. Let's implement:
```
def pair_line_search(h,n,i,j,best_c):
    oi=h[i]; oj=h[j]
    lo=max(-oi, oj-1.0)
    hi=min(oj, 1.0-oi)
    if hi-lo < 1e-11: return h, best_c, False
    # sample maybe 12 points
    ts=np.linspace(lo,hi,13)
    bc=best_c; bt=0
    for t in ts:
       h[i]=oi+t; h[j]=oj-t
       val=compute_cmax(h)
       if val<bc: bc=val; bt=t
       h[i]=oi; h[j]=oj
    # Local golden around bt with step=(hi-lo)/12 maybe, ensure includes bilateral
    span=(hi-lo)/12.0
    lo2=max(lo, bt-span); hi2=min(hi, bt+span)
    if hi2-lo2>1e-10:
       # use 16 iterations
       gr= (np.sqrt(5)-1)/2
       a=lo2; b=ri? golden formula
       # evaluate two interior, update, 20 iters
       for _ in range(20):
           c1=a+ (1-gr)*(b-a); c2=a+gr*(b-a)
           f1=eval(t=c1); f2=eval(c2)
           if f1<f2: b=c2 else a=c1
       t=(a+b)/2; val=eval; update
    if bc < best_c - 1e-14:
       h[i]=oi+bt; h[j]=oj-bt
       return h,bc,True
    return h,best_c,False
```
Need eval function `eval_cmax(h)`: `float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full')))* (2/n)`. For n=600, line search evals many. To speed, maybe cache c_full? Not across t. But okay.

Let's refine each function.

`smooth_value`:
```
def smooth_value(h,n,alpha,mu=0.0):
    c=c_full_values(h)
    m=float(c.max())
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    amax=float(z.max())
    val=m + (amax + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-amax))))) / alpha
    if mu:
        val += mu*float((h.sum()-0.5*n)**2)
    return float(val)
```
Could overflow exp? z-amax≤0. good.

`smooth_grad`:
```
def smooth_grad(h,n,alpha,mu=0.0):
    c=c_full_values(h)
    m=float(c.max())
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    amax=float(z.max())
    z=z-amax
    w=np.exp(z)
    s=w.sum()
    if s<=0: w = np.full_like(c,1.0/len(c)) # fallback
    else: w/=s
    g=grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
    if mu: g += 2*mu*(h.sum()-0.5*n)*np.ones(n)
    return g
```
`grad_from_weights` as above. Need target? n.
Potential speed: `w` length 2n-1. Loop p in range(1,n): center=n-1. We can optimize by prealloc and local variables. Use `if wcp > 1e-14 or wmn > 1e-14`. Multiplications with arrays.
```
def grad_from_weights(h,n,w):
    g=np.zeros(n)
    c=n-1
    wc=w[c]
    if wc>0:
        g += wc*(1.0-2.0*h)
    for p in range(1,n):
        wp=w[c-p]; wn=w[c+p]
        if wp<=1e-14 and wn<=1e-14: continue
        L=n-p
        hp=h[p:]
        hl=h[:L]
        if wp>1e-14:
            g[:L] += wp*(1.0-hp)
            g[p:] -= wp*hl
        if wn>1e-14:
            g[:L] -= wn*hp
            g[p:] += wn*(1.0-hl)
    return g * (2.0/n)
```
Need ensure `w` c values are probabilities over c (already dx scaled). Gradient includes dx. Good.

Derivative: ∂m/∂c_i = 1 if i in argmax (subgrad), plus (1) from terms? Let's compute correctly. φ(c)=m + (1/α) log∑ exp(α(c_j-m)). Let z_j=α(c_j-m). φ = m + (1/α)(z_max + log∑exp(z-z_max)). If max at one i0, derivative wrt c_i:
For i=i0: ∂m=1, z_i derivative α-? z_i=α(c_i - m). If only i max, ∂z_i=α-α? because m changes with c_i, so derivative of (c_i-m)=0? Actually if m=c_i, c_i-m=0; derivative 0 for non-smooth. `lbfgs_optimize`:
```
def lbfgs_optimize(h,n,deadline,maxiter=150, configs=None):
    h=project_feasible(h)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax_value(h)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    if configs is None:
        configs = [(20,0.0),(50,0.0),(100,1.0),(400,5.0),(1500,20.0),(8000,100.0),(40000,500.0)]
    for alpha,mu in configs:
       if time.time() > deadline-1: break
       # allocate maxiter maybe 100 if n large
       it = maxiter if n<=400 else int(maxiter*0.6)
       try:
          res=minimize(lbfgs_pen_obj,h,jac=lbfgs_pen_grad,args=(n,alpha,mu),method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options={'maxiter':it,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-10,'maxfun':max(10,it*8),'maxls':30})
          h2=project_feasible(res.x)
          c2=cmax_value(h2)
          if c2<best_c-1e-15:
              best_c=c2; best_h=h2.copy(); h=h2
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best_h,best_c
```
If mu=0, equality not enforced; L-BFGS may drift sum, but project after. For smooth opt maybe okay. Use sequence of mu increasing. Project each time.

For n=600, 3 configs maybe enough.

`direction_polish` uses `pair_line_search` with deadline check in loops.

```
def direction_polish(h,n,deadline,best_c,seed=0,K=5):
    h=project_feasible(h); best=best_c
    alphas=[50,200,1000,5000,20000]
    for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>=deadline: break
      round_improved=True
      count=0
      while time.time()<deadline-2 and round_improved:
         round_improved=False
         c=c_full_values(h); m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); amax=z.max(); w=np.exp(z-amax); s=w.sum(); w/=s
         g=grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
         # if gradient not informative: 
         if g.max()-g.min() < 1e-12: break
         neg_idx=np.argsort(g)[:K]
         pos_idx=np.argsort(g)[-K:][::-1]
         # To avoid random? Use list.
         for pi in pos_idx:
            for ni in neg_idx:
               if pi==ni: continue
               h2,c2=pair_line_search(h,n,int(pi),int(ni),best,deadline)
               if c2<best-1e-13:
                   best=c2; h=h2; round_improved=True
               # after updating, gradient indices may stale but okay
               if time.time()>deadline-2: break
            if time.time()>deadline-2: break
         if round_improved and time.time()>deadline-2: break
         if not round_improved:
            # random pairs 20
            rng=np.random.RandomState(seed+alpha)
            for _ in range(20):
                i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
                if i==j: continue
                h2,c2=pair_line_search(...); update
                if time>deadline: break
            # if no improvement loop break (round_improved may false-> while exits)
    return h,best
```
This can be expensive. K maybe 4.

```
def enforce_sum_exact(h,target):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    # distribute to coordinate with enough room, maybe split
    while abs(diff)>1e-14:
```

Let's write functions carefully.

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def c_full_values(h):
    n = len(h)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * (2.0 / n)

def cmax_value(h):
    if len(h)==0: return 1e9
    return float(np.max(c_full_values(h)))

def project_feasible(h, target=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0: return h
    if target is None: target = 0.5*n
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(200):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13 * max(1.0, float(n)):
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            frac = diff / tot
            if frac > 1.0: frac = 1.0
            h += frac * room
        else:
            if s <= 1e-14:
                # set h = target/n if possible
                val = target / n
                if val >=0 and val<=1:
                    h[:] = val
                break
            h *= (target / s)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    # final exact adjust
    for _ in range(1000):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            step = min(1.0 - h[idx], diff)
            h[idx] += step
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            step = min(h[idx], -diff)
            h[idx] -= step
    return h
```
Potential issue final adjust while diff slightly > room due to floating? Room computed; if due to floating all rooms zero but diff positive impossible if target≤n. With rounding okay.

`symmetrize`:
```
def symmetrize(h):
    return project_feasible(0.5*(h + h[::-1].copy()))
```

`resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h, n_new):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0:
       return np.full(n_new,0.5)
    target=0.5*n_new
    x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)*(2.0/n_new)
    h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return project_feasible(h_new,target)
```

`smooth_value`:
```
def smooth_value(h,n,alpha,mu=0.0):
    c = c_full_values(h)
    z = alpha * c
    amax = float(np.max(z))
    val = (amax + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z - amax))))) / alpha
    if mu != 0.0:
       val += mu * (float(h.sum()) - 0.5*n)**2
    return float(val)
```
What if alpha very small? We use >=10. If alpha=0 not.

`grad_from_weights` as above.
`smooth_grad`:
```
def smooth_grad(h,n,alpha,mu=0.0):
    c = c_full_values(h)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    amax = float(np.max(z))
    w = np.exp(z - amax)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0: w[:] = 1.0/len(c); s=len(c)
    else: w /= s
    g = grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
    if mu != 0.0:
       g += 2.0*mu*(float(h.sum()) - 0.5*n)*np.ones(n)
    return g
```
But if we use smooth_value without centering, derivative weights softmax α c; using `α(c-m)` same. good.

Potential overflow in `np.exp(z)`? z≤0 after amax, safe.

`lbfgs_pen_obj` and grad use smooth_value/grad. They call `c_full` each time; okay.

But maxiter moderate. For n=300, maxiter 150 okay. For n=600, line search each iter maybe 5 gradient; 150*5=750 grads; each gradient p loop 600 =>450k slice ops; maybe 5s? okay.


`slsqp_optimize`: constraints top-level.

```
def slsqp_obj(x,n,alpha): return smooth_value(x,n,alpha)
def slsqp_cons(x,n): return float(x.sum()-0.5*n)
def slsqp_jac(x,n): return np.ones(n)
def slsqp_grad(x,n,alpha): return smooth_grad(x,n,alpha,0.0)
```
SLSQP with constraints; `cons` list created inside `slsqp_optimize` with `args=(n,)` (allowed). No lambda.

`optimize_candidate`:
```
def optimize_candidate(h,n,deadline, mode='medium'):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_c = cmax_value(h)
    now = time.time()
    rem = deadline - now
    if rem < 10:
        return best_h,best_c
    # choose lbfgs fraction
    if mode == 'long':
       frac_lb=0.55; reserve=0.25
    elif mode == 'short':
       frac_lb=0.5; reserve=0.3
    else:
       frac_lb=0.5; reserve=0.35
    if n > 800: frac_lb=0.7
    t_lb = now + frac_lb*rem
    t_lb = min(t_lb, deadline - max(10.0, reserve*rem))
    if t_lb > now + 5:
       best_h,best_c = lbfgs_optimize(h,n,t_lb)
    # SLSQP if long, n<=450, time left
    if mode == 'long' and n <= 450 and time.time() < deadline - 30:
       t_sl = min(deadline - max(10.0,0.15*(deadline-time.time())), time.time()+40)
       if t_sl > time.time()+3:
          best_h,best_c = slsqp_optimize(best_h,n,t_sl,alphas=(5000,20000), maxiter=60)
    # polish until deadline-2
    if time.time() < deadline - 2:
       best_h,best_c = direction_polish(best_h,n,deadline,best_c)
    return best_h,best_c
```
The `reserve` ensures some time for polish within candidate. But direction_polish uses `deadline` (candidate deadline). Good.

`direction_polish`: Need K maybe based on n. If n large K=4 else 6.
Potential expensive; ensure not infinite.

Implementation details for line search: `cmax_value` allocates arrays. In line search many calls; can optimize by `n` known and dx. Define `cmax_fast(h,n,dx)`:
```
def cmax_fast(h,n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, 'full')))*(2.0/n)
```
Use.

`pair_line_search`:
```
def pair_line_search(h,n,i,j,best_c,deadline):
    if i == j: return h,best_c
    if time.time() > deadline: return h,best_c
    oi = float(h[i]); oj = float(h[j])
    lo = max(-oi, oj - 1.0)
    hi = min(oj, 1.0 - oi)
    if hi - lo < 1e-11: return h,best_c
    bc = best_c
    bt = 0.0
    # samples count based on range? 10
    m = 9
    ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, m + 1)
    # include 0? if not, add
    # Loop
    for t in ts:
        h[i] = oi + t; h[j] = oj - t
        val = cmax_fast(h,n)
        if val < bc:
            bc = val; bt = float(t)
        h[i] = oi; h[j] = oj
        if val < best_c * 0.9? no
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
    # golden
    span = (hi - lo)/m
    lo2 = max(lo, bt - span)
    hi2 = min(hi, bt + span)
    if hi2 - lo2 > 1e-10 and time.time() < deadline:
       a=lo2; b=hi2; gr=(np.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
       c=a+(1.0-gr)*(b-a); d=a+gr*(b-a)
       h[i]=oi+c; h[j]=oj-c; fc=cmax_fast(h,n); h[i]=oi; h[j]=oj
       h[i]=oi+d; h[j]=oj-d; fd=cmax_fast(h,n); h[i]=oi; h[j]=oj
       if fc < bc: bc=fc; bt=c
       if fd < bc: bc=fd; bt=d
       for _ in range(14):
           if time.time() > deadline: break
           if fc < fd:
               b = d; d = c; fd = fc
               c = a + (1.0 - gr)*(b - a)
               h[i]=oi+c; h[j]=oj-c; fc=cmax_fast(h,n); h[i]=oi; h[j]=oj
               if fc < bc: bc=fc; bt=c
           else:
               a = c; c = d; fc = fd
               d = a + gr*(b - a)
               h[i]=oi+d; h[j]=oj-d; fd=cmax_fast(h,n); h[i]=oi; h[j]=oj
               if fd < bc: bc=fd; bt=d
           if abs(b-a) < 1e-10: break
       tmid=0.5*(a+b); h[i]=oi+tmid; h[j]=oj-tmid; val=cmax_fast(h,n); h[i]=oi; h[j]=oj
       if val < bc: bc=val; bt=tmid
    if bc < best_c - 1e-14:
       h[i] = oi + bt; h[j] = oj - bt
       h[i] = min(1.0,max(0.0,h[i])); h[j]=min(1.0,max(0.0,h[j]))
       # ensure sum? t within bounds; may tiny rounding
       diff = 0.5*n - float(h.sum())
       # if tiny adjust j? Since moved sum unchanged, no. If clipping changed, adjust i?
       if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
           h = project_feasible(h) # but returns new array; okay? It copies. But inside direction we expect h modified. We'll assign.
       return h, bc
    h[i]=oi; h[j]=oj
    return h, best_c
```
If project_feasible returns new array, line search returns h possibly different; okay. But if clipping caused sum off, project changes other coordinates? That breaks pair move (sum). Project may adjust. Fine.

`direction_polish` with K:
```
def direction_polish(h,n,deadline,best_c,seed=0):
    h=project_feasible(h)
    best=best_c
    if n <= 400: K=6
    elif n <= 800: K=4
    else: K=3
    alphas=[50,300,2000,15000]
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() >= deadline: break
       progressed=True
       while time.time() < deadline - 2 and progressed:
          progressed=False
          c=c_full_values(h); m=c.max(); z=alpha*(c-m); amax=z.max(); w=np.exp(z-amax); s=w.sum(); w/=s
          g=grad_from_weights(h,n,w)
          if g.max() - g.min() < 1e-12: break
          neg_idx=np.argsort(g)[:K]
          pos_idx=np.argsort(g)[-K:][::-1]
          # limit pairs if time
          for pi in pos_idx:
             for ni in neg_idx:
                if pi == ni: continue
                h2,c2 = pair_line_search(h,n,int(pi),int(ni),best,deadline)
                if c2 < best - 1e-13:
                    h = h2; best = c2; progressed=True
                if time.time() > deadline - 2:
                    break
             if time.time() > deadline - 2:
                break
          if not progressed and time.time() < deadline - 2:
             rng=np.random.RandomState(seed + int(alpha))
             for _ in range(12):
                i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
                if i==j: continue
                h2,c2=pair_line_search(h,n,i,j,best,deadline)
                if c2 < best - 1e-13:
                    h=h2; best=c2; progressed=True
                if time.time() > deadline - 2: break
          # If no progress, break alpha loop to save time
          if not progressed:
             break
    return h,best
```
Alpha loop: If no progress at low alpha, maybe higher alpha? We break outer if no progress? In code above break only inner while; after while if not progressed we should break outer too. Add `advanced=False`? Simpler: At end of while, if no progress break. But if low alpha no progress, high alpha might? Could be. But to save, break. Maybe use high alpha first? Active gradient important. Use alphas high to low? Start with 10000 to find peaks, then lower. Use [10000,2000,300,50]. If no progress, lower may smooth and improve. Better keep.

If no progress at highest, random may. We'll set alphas descending: [10000,2000,300,50]. For each alpha, while progress; after each alpha if no progress, continue to next? Could break after all low. To avoid infinite long, break if no progress for two consecutive alphas? Simpler: outer for, if no progress at alpha and alpha<1000? break? Hmm. We can just let run; if no progress, each alpha does one pass then breaks; okay.

But `direction_polish` can be called often; need not exhaustive. Fine.

`fourier_search`:
```
def make_fourier_vec(n,z,n_modes=3):
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*(2.0/n)
    v=np.full(n,0.5)
    for r in range(n_modes):
        a=float(z[2*r]); ph=float(z[2*r+1])
        if a != 0:
           v += 0.5*a*np.cos((r+1)*np.pi*x + ph)
    return project_feasible(np.clip(v,0.0,1.0))
def fourier_obj(z,n,n_modes=3):
    h=make_fourier_vec(n,z,n_modes)
    return cmax_value(h)
def fourier_start(n,seed,known=True):
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=1e9
    n_modes=3
    # known config: a1=1, phase maybe optimize? We can just create several
    candidates_z=[]
    z=np.zeros(2*n_modes); z[0]=1.0; z[1]=0.0; candidates_z.append(z)
    z[0]=1.0; z[1]=np.pi/2; candidates_z.append(z)
    for _ in range(4):
       z = np.concatenate((rng.rand(n_modes)*0.8, rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,size=n_modes)))
       candidates_z.append(z)
    for z in candidates_z:
       h=make_fourier_vec(n,z,n_modes); c=cmax_value(h)
       if c<best_c: best_h=h; best_c=c
    return best_h,best_c
```
Then in run, if time, run Nelder-Mead on best z? This adds code. Maybe not needed. Instead generate 5 Fourier starts and feed into `optimize_candidate` with short mode. But `optimize_candidate` will run full L-BFGS; random Fourier may improve. Time limited.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t_start=time.time()
    global_deadline=t_start+budget_s-8.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    # parent
    n0=300
    try:
       hpar=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n0=len(hpar)
       if n0 ==0: raise
    except Exception:
       hpar=np.full(300,0.5)
       n0=300
    hpar=project_feasible(hpar)
    best_h=hpar.copy(); best_c=cmax_value(best_h)
    # quick sym candidate?
    hs=symmetrize(hpar); cs=cmax_value(hs)
    if cs < best_c: best...
    # main optimize parent n0 with mode long, deadline = global_deadline - reserve
    reserve = 350.0 if n0 <=400 else 250.0
    t_main = min(global_deadline - max(50.0, reserve), t_start + 420.0)
    if t_main > t_start + 20:
         h1,c1=optimize_candidate(best_h,n0,t_main,mode='long')
         if c1<best_c: best...
    # high resolutions
    n_candidates=[]
    if n0 <= 500:
       n2 = 2*n0
       if n2%2: n2-=1
       if n2 > n0 and n2 <= 1000: n_candidates.append(n2)
       n3 = int(round(1.5*n0))
       if n3%2: n3-=1
       if n3 > n0 and n3 not in n_candidates: n_candidates.append(n3)
    for n_new in n_candidates[:2]:
       if time.time() > global_deadline - 80: break
       h_interp=resample_h(best_h,n_new)
       # maybe also sym interp
       alloc = min(160.0, global_deadline - time.time() - 50.0)
       if alloc < 30: break
       t_end = time.time() + alloc
       h2,c2=optimize_candidate(h_interp,n_new,t_end,mode='medium')
       if c2<best_c: best...
       # if best n changed continue intersampling?
    # Fourier/random starts if time
    if time.time() < global_deadline - 120:
       n_four = n0 if n0<=500 else 300
       h_four,c_four=fourier_start(n_four,seed)
       # do a short optimize
       alloc=min(100.0, global_deadline-time.time()-50.0)
       if alloc>20:
          h4,c4=optimize_candidate(h_four,n_four,time.time()+alloc,mode='short')
          if c4<best_c: best...
       # random perturb of best
       h_rand=project_feasible(best_h + 0.05*rng.randn(len(best_h)))
       if time.time() < global_deadline - 50:
           alloc=min(60.0, global_deadline-time.time()-30.0)
           if alloc>15:
              hr,cr=optimize_candidate(h_rand,len(best_h),time.time()+alloc,mode='short')
              if cr<best_c: best...
    # final polish
    nf=len(best_h)
    if time.time() < global_deadline - 2:
       # if nf<=350 and time enough, SLSQP quick
       if nf<=350 and time.time() < global_deadline - 20:
           best_h,best_c=slsqp_optimize(best_h,nf,min(global_deadline-10, time.time()+30), alphas=(20000,100000), maxiter=50)
       best_h,best_c=direction_polish(best_h,nf,global_deadline,best_c,seed+1)
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h)
    best_c=cmax_value(best_h)
    return best_h, float(best_c), len(best_h)
```

```
def slsqp_optimize(h,n,deadline,alphas=(5000,20000),maxiter=60):
    h=project_feasible(h); best...
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':slsqp_cons,'jac':slsqp_jac,'args':(n,)}]

          res = minimize(slsqp_obj, h, args=(n,alpha), jac=slsqp_grad, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-14})

       except: pass
```

```
def fourier_nelder(n, deadline, seed=0):
    n_modes=3
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=1e9
    starts=[]
    z=np.zeros(2*n_modes); z[0]=1.0; z[1]=0.0; z[2]=0.2; z[3]=rng.uniform(-1,1); z[4]=0.1; z[5]=rng.uniform(-1,1); starts.append(z)
    z=... phase pi/2
    for _ in range(4): z = np.concatenate([rng.rand(n_modes)*0.7, rng.uniform(-np.pi,np.pi,size=n_modes)]); starts.append(z)
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem<5: return make_fourier direct eval?
    per=rem/len(starts)
    # simplex init optional? default size okay. maxiter max(50, int(per*5)) 
    for idx,z0 in enumerate(starts):
       t_end=time.time()+per
       maxit=int(max(50, min(800, per*30)))
       try:
          res=minimize(fourier_obj,z0,args=(n,n_modes),method='Nelder-Mead',options={'maxiter':maxit,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-9,'adaptive':True})
          h=make_fourier_vec(n,res.x,n_modes); c=cmax_value(h)
          update
       except:
          h=make_fourier_vec(n,z0,n_modes); c=...
       # also evaluate z0
       if time.time()>deadline: break
    if best_h is None: return ...
    return best_h,best_c
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


def c_full_values(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(1)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / n)


def cmax_value(h):
    if len(h) == 0:
        return 1e9
    return float(np.max(c_full_values(h)))


def cmax_fast(h, n):
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full"))) * (2.0 / n)


def project_feasible(h, target=None):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float).copy()
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return h
    if target is None:
        target = 0.5 * n

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(250):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-13 * max(1.0, float(n)):
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot <= 1e-14:
                break
            frac = diff / tot
            if frac > 1.0:
                frac = 1.0
            h += frac * room
        else:
            if s <= 1e-14:
                val = target / n
                if 0.0 <= val <= 1.0:
                    h[:] = val
                break
            h *= (target / s)

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)

    for _ in range(2000):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            step = min(float(1.0 - h[idx]), diff)
            if step <= 0:
                break
            h[idx] += step
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            step = min(float(h[idx]), -diff)
            if step <= 0:
                break
            h[idx] -= step

    return h


def symmetrize(h):
    return project_feasible(0.5 * (h + h[::-1].copy()))


def resample_h(h, n_new):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return np.full(n_new, 0.5)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    target = 0.5 * n_new
    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n_new)
    h_new = np.interp(x_new, x_old, h)
    return project_feasible(h_new, target)


def smooth_value(x, n, alpha, mu=0.0):
    c = c_full_values(x)
    z = alpha * c
    amax = float(np.max(z))
    val = (amax + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z - amax))))) / alpha
    if mu != 0.0:
        val += mu * (float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n) ** 2
    return float(val)


def grad_from_weights(h, n, w):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    center = n - 1

    wc = float(w[center])
    if wc > 1e-16:
        g += wc * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)

    for p in range(1, n):
        wp = float(w[center - p])
        wn = float(w[center + p])
        if wp <= 1e-14 and wn <= 1e-14:
            continue
        L = n - p
        hp = h[p:]
        hl = h[:L]
        if wp > 1e-14:
            g[:L] += wp * (1.0 - hp)
            g[p:] -= wp * hl
        if wn > 1e-14:
            g[:L] -= wn * hp
            g[p:] += wn * (1.0 - hl)

    g *= (2.0 / n)
    return g


def smooth_grad(x, n, alpha, mu=0.0):
    c = c_full_values(x)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    amax = float(np.max(z))
    w = np.exp(z - amax)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        w[:] = 1.0 / len(c)
        s = float(len(c))
    else:
        w /= s

    g = grad_from_weights(x, n, w)
    if mu != 0.0:
        g += 2.0 * mu * (float(x.sum()) - 0.5 * n) * np.ones(n)
    return g


def lbfgs_pen_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    return smooth_value(x, n, alpha, mu)


def lbfgs_pen_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    return smooth_grad(x, n, alpha, mu)


def lbfgs_optimize(h, n, deadline):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax_value(h)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n

    if n <= 350:
        configs = [
            (50.0, 5.0),
            (200.0, 10.0),
            (1000.0, 50.0),
            (4000.0, 100.0),
            (15000.0, 500.0),
            (60000.0, 2000.0),
        ]
        it = 120
    elif n <= 750:
        configs = [
            (50.0, 10.0),
            (200.0, 50.0),
            (1000.0, 100.0),
            (5000.0, 500.0),
            (20000.0, 2000.0),
        ]
        it = 80
    else:
        configs = [
            (50.0, 50.0),
            (200.0, 200.0),
            (1000.0, 500.0),
        ]
        it = 50

    for alpha, mu in configs:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                lbfgs_pen_obj,
                h,
                jac=lbfgs_pen_grad,
                args=(n, alpha, mu),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": it,
                    "ftol": 1e-15,
                    "gtol": 1e-11,
                    "maxfun": max(20, 8 * it),
                    "maxls": 30,
                },
            )
            h2 = project_feasible(res.x)
            c2 = cmax_value(h2)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2
                h = h2
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, best_c


def slsqp_obj(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_value(x, n, alpha, 0.0)


def slsqp_cons(x, n):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * n)


def slsqp_jac(x, n):
    return np.ones(n)


def slsqp_grad(x, n, alpha):
    return smooth_grad(x, n, alpha, 0.0)


def slsqp_optimize(h, n, deadline, alphas=(5000.0, 20000.0, 100000.0), maxiter=60):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax_value(h)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [
        {
            "type": "eq",
            "fun": slsqp_cons,
            "jac": slsqp_jac,
            "args": (n,),
        }
    ]

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        try:
            res = minimize(
                slsqp_obj,
                h,
                args=(n, alpha),
                jac=slsqp_grad,
                method="SLSQP",
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-14},
            )
            h2 = project_feasible(res.x)
            c2 = cmax_value(h2)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c = c2
                best_h = h2
                h = h2
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_h, best_c


def pair_set(h, n, i, j, oi, oj, t):
    h[i] = oi + t
    h[j] = oj - t


def pair_eval(h, n, i, j, oi, oj, t):
    h[i] = oi + t
    h[j] = oj - t
    v = cmax_fast(h, n)
    h[i] = oi
    h[j] = oj
    return v


def pair_line_search(h, n, i, j, best_c, deadline):
    if i == j:
        return h, best_c
    if time.time() > deadline:
        return h, best_c

    oi = float(h[i])
    oj = float(h[j])

    lo = max(-oi, oj - 1.0)
    hi = min(oj, 1.0 - oi)
    if hi - lo < 1e-11:
        return h, best_c

    bc = best_c
    bt = 0.0

    m = 9
    ts = np.linspace(lo, hi, m + 1)
    for t in ts:
        t = float(t)
        val = pair_eval(h, n, i, j, oi, oj, t)
        if val < bc:
            bc = val
            bt = t
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

    span = (hi - lo) / m
    lo2 = max(lo, bt - span)
    hi2 = min(hi, bt + span)

    if hi2 - lo2 > 1e-10 and time.time() < deadline:
        a = lo2
        b = hi2
        gr = (np.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0

        c = a + (1.0 - gr) * (b - a)
        d = a + gr * (b - a)

        fc = pair_eval(h, n, i, j, oi, oj, c)
        fd = pair_eval(h, n, i, j, oi, oj, d)

        if fc < bc:
            bc = fc
            bt = c
        if fd < bc:
            bc = fd
            bt = d

        for _ in range(16):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            if fc < fd:
                b = d
                d = c
                fd = fc
                c = a + (1.0 - gr) * (b - a)
                fc = pair_eval(h, n, i, j, oi, oj, c)
                if fc < bc:
                    bc = fc
                    bt = c
            else:
                a = c
                c = d
                fc = fd
                d = a + gr * (b - a)
                fd = pair_eval(h, n, i, j, oi, oj, d)
                if fd < bc:
                    bc = fd
                    bt = d

            if abs(b - a) < 1e-11:
                break

        tm = 0.5 * (a + b)
        vm = pair_eval(h, n, i, j, oi, oj, tm)
        if vm < bc:
            bc = vm
            bt = tm

    if bc < best_c - 1e-14:
        h[i] = oi + bt
        h[j] = oj - bt
        h[i] = min(1.0, max(0.0, h[i]))
        h[j] = min(1.0, max(0.0, h[j]))
        if abs(float(h.sum()) - 0.5 * n) > 1e-12:
            h = project_feasible(h)
        return h, bc

    h[i] = oi
    h[j] = oj
    return h, best_c


def direction_polish(h, n, deadline, best_c, seed=0):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    best = best_c

    if n <= 450:
        K = 6
    elif n <= 850:
        K = 4
    else:
        K = 3

    alphas = [10000.0, 2000.0, 300.0, 50.0]

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() >= deadline:
            break
        progressed = True

        while time.time() < deadline - 2.0 and progressed:
            progressed = False

            c = c_full_values(h)
            m = float(np.max(c))
            z = alpha * (c - m)
            amax = float(np.max(z))
            w = np.exp(z - amax)
            s = float(w.sum())
            if s <= 0.0:
                w[:] = 1.0 / len(c)
                s = float(len(c))
            else:
                w /= s

            g = grad_from_weights(h, n, w)

            if float(np.max(g) - np.min(g)) < 1e-12:
                break

            neg_idx = np.argsort(g)[:K]
            pos_idx = np.argsort(g)[-K:][::-1]

            for pi in pos_idx:
                for ni in neg_idx:
                    if pi == ni:
                        continue
                    h2, c2 = pair_line_search(h, n, int(pi), int(ni), best, deadline)
                    if c2 < best - 1e-13:
                        h = h2
                        best = c2
                        progressed = True
                    if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                        break
                if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                    break

            if not progressed and time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
                rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + int(alpha))
                for _ in range(12):
                    i = int(rng.randint(n))
                    j = int(rng.randint(n))
                    if i == j:
                        continue
                    h2, c2 = pair_line_search(h, n, i, j, best, deadline)
                    if c2 < best - 1e-13:
                        h = h2
                        best = c2
                        progressed = True
                    if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
                        break

            if not progressed:
                break

    return h, best


def optimize_candidate(h, n, deadline, mode="medium"):
    h = project_feasible(h)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = cmax_value(h)

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem < 10.0:
        return best_h, best_c

    if mode == "long":
        lbfrac = 0.52
        reserve_frac = 0.28
    elif mode == "short":
        lbfrac = 0.48
        reserve_frac = 0.30
    else:
        lbfrac = 0.50
        reserve_frac = 0.34

    if n > 850:
        lbfrac = 0.70
        reserve_frac = 0.18

    t_lbfgs = time.time() + lbfrac * rem
    t_lbfgs = min(t_lbfgs, deadline - max(10.0, reserve_frac * rem))

    if t_lbfgs > time.time() + 5.0:
        best_h, best_c = lbfgs_optimize(best_h, n, t_lbfgs)

    sym = symmetrize(best_h)
    cs = cmax_value(sym)
    if cs < best_c - 1e-15:
        best_h = sym
        best_c = cs

    if (
        mode == "long"
        and n <= 450
        and time.time() < deadline - 30.0
    ):
        extra = min(45.0, max(0.0, 0.15 * (deadline - time.time())))
        if extra > 5.0:
            t_sl = time.time() + extra
            t_sl = min(t_sl, deadline - 10.0)
            best_h, best_c = slsqp_optimize(
                best_h,
                n,
                t_sl,
                alphas=(5000.0, 20000.0),
                maxiter=55,
            )

    if time.time() < deadline - 2.0:
        best_h, best_c = direction_polish(best_h, n, deadline, best_c)

    return best_h, best_c


def make_fourier_vec(n, z, n_modes=3):
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    v = np.full(n, 0.5)
    for r in range(n_modes):
        a = float(z[2 * r])
        ph = float(z[2 * r + 1])
        if a != 0.0:
            v += 0.5 * a * np.cos((r + 1) * np.pi * x + ph)
    return project_feasible(np.clip(v, 0.0, 1.0))


def fourier_obj(z, n, n_modes=3):
    h = make_fourier_vec(n, z, n_modes)
    return cmax_value(h)


def fourier_start(n, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n_modes = 3
    starts = []

    z = np.zeros(2 * n_modes)
    z[0] = 1.0
    z[1] = 0.0
    z[2] = 0.25
    z[3] = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
    z[4] = 0.12
    z[5] = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
    starts.append(z)

    z = np.zeros(2 * n_modes)
    z[0] = 1.0
    z[1] = np.pi / 2.0
    z[2] = 0.20
    z[3] = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
    z[4] = 0.10
    z[5] = rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0)
    starts.append(z)

    for _ in range(4):
        amps = rng.rand(n_modes) * 0.75
        phs = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=n_modes)
        starts.append(np.concatenate((amps, phs)))

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e9
    for z in starts:
        h = make_fourier_vec(n, z, n_modes)
        c = cmax_value(h)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = h

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = np.full(n, 0.5)
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h)
        best_c = cmax_value(best_h)

    return best_h, best_c


def fourier_nelder(n, deadline, seed=0):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    n_modes = 3

    starts = []
    z = np.zeros(2 * n_modes)
    z[0] = 1.0
    z[1] = 0.1
    z[2] = 0.25
    z[3] = rng.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
    z[4] = 0.12
    z[5] = rng.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
    starts.append(z.copy())

    z[:] = 0.0
    z[0] = 1.0
    z[1] = np.pi / 2.0
    z[2] = 0.22
    z[3] = rng.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
    z[4] = 0.10
    z[5] = rng.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
    starts.append(z.copy())

    for _ in range(4):
        amps = rng.rand(n_modes) * 0.75
        phs = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=n_modes)
        starts.append(np.concatenate((amps, phs)))

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem < 5.0 or not starts:
        h, c = fourier_start(n, seed)
        return h, c

    per = rem / float(len(starts))
    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e9

    for idx, z0 in enumerate(starts):
        h0 = make_fourier_vec(n, z0, n_modes)
        c0 = cmax_value(h0)
        if c0 < best_c:
            best_c = c0
            best_h = h0

        if time.time() > deadline:
            break

        t_end = time.time() + per
        maxit = int(max(80, min(900, per * 35)))
        try:
            res = minimize(
                fourier_obj,
                z0,
                args=(n, n_modes),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxit,
                    "xatol": 1e-6,
                    "fatol": 1e-9,
                    "adaptive": True,
                },
            )
            h1 = make_fourier_vec(n, res.x, n_modes)
            c1 = cmax_value(h1)
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_c = c1
                best_h = h1
        except Exception:
            pass

    if best_h is None:
        h, c = fourier_start(n, seed)
        return h, c

    return best_h, best_c


def blend_value(h1, h2, t):
    ht = (1.0 - t) * h1 + t * h2
    ht = project_feasible(ht)
    return cmax_value(ht)


def blend_pair(h1, h2, deadline):
    n = len(h1)
    h1 = project_feasible(h1)
    h2 = project_feasible(h2)
    if len(h2) != n:
        h2 = resample_h(h2, n)

    a = 0.0
    b = 1.0
    gr = (np.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0

    c = a + (1.0 - gr) * (b - a)
    d = a + gr * (b - a)
    fc = blend_value(h1, h2, c)
    fd = blend_value(h1, h2, d)

    best_t = 0.0
    best_c = cmax_value(h1)
    if fc < best_c:
        best_c = fc
        best_t = c
    if fd < best_c:
        best_c = fd
        best_t = d

    for _ in range(22):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if fc < fd:
            b = d
            d = c
            fd = fc
            c = a + (1.0 - gr) * (b - a)
            fc = blend_value(h1, h2, c)
        else:
            a = c
            c = d
            fc = fd
            d = a + gr * (b - a)
            fd = blend_value(h1, h2, d)

        if fc < best_c:
            best_c = fc
            best_t = c
        if fd < best_c:
            best_c = fd
            best_t = d

        if abs(b - a) < 1e-9:
            break

    tm = 0.5 * (a + b)
    vm = blend_value(h1, h2, tm)
    if vm < best_c:
        best_c = vm
        best_t = tm

    h_out = (1.0 - best_t) * h1 + best_t * h2
    h_out = project_feasible(h_out)
    c_out = cmax_value(h_out)
    if c_out < cmax_value(h1):
        return h_out, c_out
    return h1, cmax_value(h1)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t_start = time.time()
    global_deadline = t_start + float(budget_s) - 8.0

    try:
        hpar = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        npar = len(hpar)
        if npar == 0:
            raise ValueError("empty parent")
    except Exception:
        hpar = np.full(300, 0.5)
        npar = 300

    hpar = project_feasible(hpar)
    best_h = hpar.copy()
    best_c = cmax_value(best_h)

    hs = symmetrize(hpar)
    cs = cmax_value(hs)
    if cs < best_c:
        best_h = hs
        best_c = cs

    if npar <= 650:
        n_main = npar
    else:
        n_main = 400
        hpar = project_feasible(resample_h(hpar, n_main))

    h_main = hpar.copy()
    cm = cmax_value(h_main)
    if cm < best_c:
        best_h = h_main
        best_c = cm

    if time.time() < global_deadline - 300.0:
        t_main = min(global_deadline - 320.0, t_start + 320.0)
        if t_main > time.time() + 20.0:
            h1, c1 = optimize_candidate(h_main, n_main, t_main, mode="long")
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_h = h1
                best_c = c1

    n_candidates = []
    if n_main <= 520:
        n2 = 2 * n_main
        if n2 % 2:
            n2 -= 1
        if n2 > n_main and n2 <= 1050:
            n_candidates.append(n2)
        n3 = int(round(1.5 * n_main))
        if n3 % 2:
            n3 -= 1
        if n3 > n_main and n3 not in n_candidates:
            n_candidates.append(n3)

    for n_new in n_candidates[:2]:
        if time.time() > global_deadline - 70.0:
            break
        alloc = min(170.0, global_deadline - time.time() - 70.0)
        if alloc < 35.0:
            break
        h_interp = resample_h(best_h, n_new)
        t_end = time.time() + alloc
        h2, c2 = optimize_candidate(h_interp, n_new, t_end, mode="medium")
        if c2 < best_c:
            best_h = h2
            best_c = c2

    if time.time() < global_deadline - 160.0:
        n_four = n_main if n_main <= 500 else 300
        h_four, c_four = fourier_start(n_four, seed)
        if c_four < best_c:
            best_h = h_four
            best_c = c_four

        alloc = min(90.0, global_deadline - time.time() - 90.0)
        if alloc > 25.0:
            h4, c4 = optimize_candidate(h_four, n_four, time.time() + alloc, mode="short")
            if c4 < best_c:
                best_h = h4
                best_c = c4

        if time.time() < global_deadline - 90.0:
            alloc2 = min(70.0, global_deadline - time.time() - 60.0)
            if alloc2 > 20.0:
                hn, cn = fourier_nelder(n_four, time.time() + alloc2, seed + 7)
                if cn < best_c:
                    best_h = hn
                    best_c = cn

                if time.time() < global_deadline - 40.0:
                    bb, cb = blend_pair(best_h, best_h if cn >= best_c else hn, global_deadline)
                    if cb < best_c:
                        best_h = bb
                        best_c = cb

    if time.time() < global_deadline - 60.0:
        blend_src = best_h.copy()
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed + 101)
        blend_dst = project_feasible(blend_src + 0.06 * rng.randn(len(blend_src)))
        alloc = min(70.0, global_deadline - time.time() - 50.0)
        if alloc > 20.0:
            hb, cb = blend_pair(blend_src, blend_dst, time.time() + alloc)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h = hb
                best_c = cb
            hopt, copt = optimize_candidate(hb, len(hb), time.time() + min(45.0, alloc * 0.6), mode="short")
            if copt < best_c:
                best_h = hopt
                best_c = copt

    nfinal = len(best_h)
    if time.time() < global_deadline - 20.0:
        if nfinal <= 380:
            t_sl = min(global_deadline - 12.0, time.time() + 35.0)
            if t_sl > time.time() + 5.0:
                best_h, best_c = slsqp_optimize(
                    best_h,
                    nfinal,
                    t_sl,
                    alphas=(20000.0, 100000.0),
                    maxiter=45,
                )

    if time.time() < global_deadline - 2.0:
        best_h, best_c = direction_polish(best_h, nfinal, global_deadline, best_c, seed=97)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h)
    best_c = float(cmax_value(best_h))
    nfinal = len(best_h)
    return best_h, best_c, nfinal
```
